    <script type="text/javascript" id="klevu_initsessiondata">
        var nowUnixtime = parseInt(Date.now() / 1000);

        function klevufejs_getCookie(name) {
            if (typeof (name) === "undefined") {
                name = "klv_mage";
            }
            var c = "",
                ca = document.cookie.split(';');

            for (var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
                c = ca[i];
                if (typeof c !== "string") {
                    continue;
                }
                var cookiePair = c.split("=");

                if (name === cookiePair[0].trim()) {
                    try {
                        return JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(cookiePair[1]));
                    } catch (err) {
                        // this is fine, data will be regenerated
                    }
                }
            }
            return {
                expire_sections: {}
            };
        }

        document.addEventListener('klevu.customerData.loaded', function (e) {
            var klevufejs_cookie = klevufejs_getCookie();
            klevufejs_cookie.expire_sections.customerData = nowUnixtime + 600;

            document.cookie = "klv_mage=" + JSON.stringify(klevufejs_cookie) + ";" + (new Date((nowUnixtime + 3600) * 1000).toUTCString()) + ";path=/;SameSite=Strict";
        });

        var klevufejs_cookie = klevufejs_getCookie();
        var klevuData = {
            ...{
                customerData: {
                    revalidate_after: -1
                }
            },
            ...JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem('klv_mage') || '{}')
        };

        const klevuCustomerDataLoadedEvent = document.createEvent('CustomEvent');
        klevuCustomerDataLoadedEvent.initEvent('klevu.customerData.loaded', false, true);
        const klevuCustomerDataLoadErrorEvent = document.createEvent('CustomEvent');
        klevuCustomerDataLoadErrorEvent.initEvent('klevu.customerData.loadError', false, true);

        if (typeof klevufejs_cookie.expire_sections !== "object"
            || (klevufejs_cookie.expire_sections.customerData || -1) < nowUnixtime
            || klevuData.customerData.revalidate_after < nowUnixtime
        ) {
            var xhttp = new XMLHttpRequest();
            xhttp.onerror = function (request) {
                document.dispatchEvent(klevuCustomerDataLoadErrorEvent);
            };
            xhttp.ontimeout = function (request) {
                this.onerror(request);
            };
            xhttp.onload = function (request) {
                if (this.status >= 400 || this.timeout) {
                    this.onerror(request);
                    return;
                }

                var klevuData = JSON.parse(window.localStorage.getItem('klv_mage') || '{}');
                klevuData.customerData = JSON.parse(this.response);
                window.localStorage.setItem('klv_mage', JSON.stringify(klevuData));

                document.dispatchEvent(klevuCustomerDataLoadedEvent);
            };
            xhttp.open('GET', 'https://www.mothercare.co.id/rest/V1/klevu/customerData', false);
            xhttp.send();
        } else {
            document.dispatchEvent(klevuCustomerDataLoadedEvent);
        }
    </script>

    <!-- Floating Clouds Effect: overlay visual awan melayang di langit -->
    <style id="floating-clouds-effect">
        #floating-clouds-layer {
            position: fixed;
            inset: 0;
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            overflow: hidden;
            pointer-events: none;
            z-index: 2147483000;
            isolation: isolate
        }

        .sky-cloud {
            display: block;
            position: absolute;
            pointer-events: none;
            opacity: 0;
            will-change: transform, opacity;
            filter: blur(2px);
            animation: cloudDrift var(--cloud-speed) linear var(--cloud-delay) infinite
        }

        .sky-cloud::before {
            content: "";
            position: absolute;
            width: 100%;
            height: 100%;
            border-radius: 50% 50% 45% 45%;
            background: var(--cloud-color);
            box-shadow:
                calc(var(--cloud-size) * .45) calc(var(--cloud-size) * .08) 0 calc(var(--cloud-size) * -.12) var(--cloud-color),
                calc(var(--cloud-size) * -.35) calc(var(--cloud-size) * .06) 0 calc(var(--cloud-size) * -.1) var(--cloud-color),
                calc(var(--cloud-size) * .2) calc(var(--cloud-size) * -.18) 0 calc(var(--cloud-size) * .02) var(--cloud-color2),
                calc(var(--cloud-size) * -.15) calc(var(--cloud-size) * -.12) 0 calc(var(--cloud-size) * -.04) var(--cloud-color2)
        }

        @keyframes cloudDrift {
            0% {
                transform: translate3d(-20vw, var(--cloud-y), 0) scaleX(var(--cloud-scaleX));
                opacity: 0;
            }

            5% {
                opacity: var(--cloud-opacity);
            }

            90% {
                opacity: var(--cloud-opacity);
            }

            100% {
                transform: translate3d(120vw, var(--cloud-y), 0) scaleX(var(--cloud-scaleX));
                opacity: 0;
            }
        }

        @media (max-width:768px) {
            .sky-cloud {
                filter: blur(.5px)
            }
        }

        @media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce) {
            #floating-clouds-layer {
                display: none !important
            }
        }
    </style>

                                <script>
                                    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',
                                        function () {
                                            const faqItems = document.querySelectorAll('.faq-item');
                                            faqItems.forEach(item => {
                                                const question = item.querySelector('.faq-question');
                                                question.addEventListener('click',
                                                    () => {
                                                        const isActive = item.classList.contains('active');
                                                        faqItems.forEach(otherItem => { otherItem.classList.remove('active') });
                                                        if (!isActive) { item.classList.add('active') }
                                                    })
                                            })
                                        });
                                </script>

        <script>document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () { const parade = document.getElementById("viking-parade"); const vikingImage = "https://imgaku.com/images/lt-icon-baru.webp"; const totalVikings = 7; for (let i = 0; i < totalVikings; i++) { const viking = document.createElement("img"); viking.src = vikingImage; viking.alt = ""; viking.className = "viking-runner"; const speed = 13 + Math.random() * 7; const delay = -(i * 2.8); const bounceDelay = -(Math.random() * 0.5); viking.style.setProperty("--speed", speed + "s"); viking.style.setProperty("--delay", delay + "s"); viking.style.setProperty("--bounce-delay", bounceDelay + "s"); viking.style.width = (115 + Math.random() * 35) + "px"; parade.appendChild(viking); } });</script>

    <script id="floating-clouds-script">(function () {
            function initFloatingClouds() {
                if (window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches) return;
                if (document.getElementById('floating-clouds-layer')) return;
                const layer = document.createElement('div');
                layer.id = 'floating-clouds-layer';
                layer.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
                const palette = [
                    ['rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9)', 'rgba(240, 240, 250, 0.7)'],
                    ['rgba(255, 245, 248, 0.85)', 'rgba(255, 230, 235, 0.65)'],
                    ['rgba(250, 250, 255, 0.8)', 'rgba(245, 240, 248, 0.6)'],
                    ['rgba(255, 252, 240, 0.75)', 'rgba(255, 248, 235, 0.6)'],
                    ['rgba(255, 240, 245, 0.85)', 'rgba(255, 235, 240, 0.65)'],
                    ['rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95)', 'rgba(248, 248, 255, 0.7)']
                ];
                const count = window.innerWidth < 600 ? 8 : 14;
                const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
                for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                    const cloud = document.createElement('span');
                    cloud.className = 'sky-cloud';
                    const colors = palette[Math.floor(Math.random() * palette.length)];
                    const size = 50 + Math.random() * 90;
                    const speed = 40 + Math.random() * 55;
                    const delay = -(Math.random() * speed);
                    const y = 2 + Math.random() * 50;
                    const opacity = 0.35 + Math.random() * 0.4;
                    const scaleX = 2.0 + Math.random() * 2.0;
                    cloud.style.setProperty('--cloud-color', colors[0]);
                    cloud.style.setProperty('--cloud-color2', colors[1]);
                    cloud.style.setProperty('--cloud-size', size + 'px');
                    cloud.style.setProperty('--cloud-speed', speed + 's');
                    cloud.style.setProperty('--cloud-delay', delay + 's');
                    cloud.style.setProperty('--cloud-y', y + 'vh');
                    cloud.style.setProperty('--cloud-opacity', opacity.toFixed(2));
                    cloud.style.setProperty('--cloud-scaleX', scaleX.toFixed(2));
                    cloud.style.width = size + 'px';
                    cloud.style.height = (size * 0.5) + 'px';
                    frag.appendChild(cloud);
                }
                layer.appendChild(frag);
                document.body.appendChild(layer);
            }
            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initFloatingClouds, { once: true });
            } else {
                initFloatingClouds();
            }
        })();</script>
